Frame generation 2: game and Streamline log lines around F11 presses and resizes (pulled 2026-10-27, no game launch)

Sources (on the PC, in $T\fg2): step2-latest.log (DLSS, 22:30-22:47), step4-latest.log (FSR, 22:51-23:04), sl-step2.log
(Streamline's own log for step 2). Paths under the user folder are shortened to %USERPROFILE%; nothing else is changed.

How the windows were picked:
- every 'Frame generation: off, the window is resizing' line: 10 s before to 20 s after it;
- F11 itself writes no log line. In step 2, Streamline's G-SYNC state check ('RSYNC: GSYNC FS OFF is 1') flips three
  times with no resize, minimise or frame generation toggle line within 1 s (22:39:27.094, 22:40:57.703, 22:40:58.884).
  Each is an F11 press that snapped back to full screen or an Alt-Tab (both were done then; the log can't tell them
  apart), and gets the same window. The flips at 22:43:27, 22:44:03 and 22:44:31 are frame generation turning on
  (the swap chain rebuild), so they don't count. Step 4 is FSR (no Streamline), so it has no such signal and only its
  resize lines count; Emre reported F11 failing there at about 22:57:14.
- overlapping windows are merged. Continuation lines (stack traces) take the time of the line above them.
Then every line of either log containing fullscreen, Couldn't toggle, GLFW, Exception or iconif (any case), and every
line of sl-step2.log containing 'ullscreen', 'ResizeTarget', 'WindowAssociation' or 'window'. Each line keeps its line
number in its file.
